GInput

form-input · ./stories/input.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GInput } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Models

Two-way bindings. Bind each one with the `v-model` syntax shown next to it — do not pass the prop and listen to its `update:` event separately.

```
export type GInputModels = {
  /**
   * @description binding value
   * @default ''
   */
  modelValue?: string | number | null; // v-model="..."
}
```

## Props

```
export type GInputProps = {
  /**
   * @description native input id
   * @default undefined
   */
  id?: string;
  /**
   * help text for input
   *
   * @default undefined
   */
  helpText?: string;
  /** @default false */
  loading?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description label text
   * @default undefined
   */
  label?: string;
  /**
   * @description message error
   * @default undefined
   */
  messageError?: string;
  /** @description whether to disable */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description same as `maxlength` in native input
   * @default 50
   */
  maxlength?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description type of input
   * @default 'text'
   */
  type?: string;
  /**
   * @description native input autocomplete
   * @default 'off'
   */
  autocomplete?: string;
  /** @description native input readonly */
  readonly?: boolean;
  /** @description toggleable password input */
  showPassword?: boolean;
  /** @description word count */
  showWordLimit?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description suffix icon
   * @default ''
   */
  suffixIcon?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description prefix icon
   * @default ''
   */
  prefixIcon?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description container role, internal properties provided for use by the picker component
   * @default undefined
   */
  containerRole?: string;
  /**
   * @description input tabindex
   * @default 0
   */
  tabindex?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description whether to trigger form validation
   * @default true
   */
  validateEvent?: boolean;
  /** @default false */
  isEvent?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description input or textarea element style
   * @default mutable({} as const)
   */
  inputStyle?: StyleValue;
  /** @description native input autofocus */
  autofocus?: boolean;
  /** @description same as `minlength` in native input */
  minlength?: string | number;
  /** @description format content */
  formatter?: Function;
  /** @description parse content */
  parser?: Function;
  /** @description placeholder */
  placeholder?: string;
  /** @description native input form */
  form?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
}
```

## Events

```
export type GInputEvents = {
  "update:modelValue": [value: string];
  click: [evt: MouseEvent];
  input: [value: string];
  change: [value: string];
  focus: [evt: FocusEvent];
  blur: [evt: FocusEvent];
  clear: [];
  mouseleave: [evt: MouseEvent];
  mouseenter: [evt: MouseEvent];
  keydown: [evt: Event | KeyboardEvent];
  compositionstart: [evt: CompositionEvent];
  compositionupdate: [evt: CompositionEvent];
  compositionend: [evt: CompositionEvent];
}
```

## Slots

Each slot is typed with the props it passes to its content.

```
export type GInputSlots = {
  prefix: {};
  suffix: {};
  helpText: { error: string | undefined; isError: boolean; };
}
```

## Exposed

Available on the component instance through a template ref.

```
export type GInputExposed = {
  /** @description is input composing */
  isComposing: boolean;
  /** @description HTML input element native method */
  focus: () => void | undefined;
  /** @description HTML input element native method */
  blur: () => void | undefined;
  /** @description HTML input element native method */
  select: () => void;
  /** @description clear input value */
  clear: () => void;
}
```

GInputNumber

form-input-number · ./stories/input-number.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GInputNumber } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Models

Two-way bindings. Bind each one with the `v-model` syntax shown next to it — do not pass the prop and listen to its `update:` event separately.

```
export type GInputNumberModels = {
  /** @description binding value */
  modelValue?: number | null; // v-model="..."
}
```

## Props

```
export type GInputNumberProps = {
  /** @description same as `id` in native input */
  id?: string;
  /** @description incremental step */
  step?: number;
  /** @description whether input value can only be multiple of step */
  stepStrictly?: boolean;
  /** @description the maximum allowed value */
  max?: number;
  /** @description the minimum allowed value */
  min?: number;
  /** @description same as `readonly` in native input */
  readonly?: boolean;
  /** @description whether the component is disabled */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** @description size of the component */
  size?: ComponentSize;
  /** @description whether to enable the control buttons */
  controls?: boolean;
  /** @description position of the control buttons */
  controlsPosition?: "" | "right";
  /** @description value should be set when input box is cleared */
  valueOnClear?: number | "max" | "min" | null;
  /** @description same as `name` in native input */
  name?: string;
  /** @description same as `placeholder` in native input */
  placeholder?: string;
  /** @description precision of input value */
  precision?: number;
  /** @description whether to trigger form validation */
  validateEvent?: boolean;
  /** @description native aria-label attribute */
  ariaLabel?: string;
  /** @description native input mode for virtual keyboards */
  inputmode?: "none" | "text" | "tel" | "url" | "email" | "numeric" | "decimal" | "search";
  /** @description alignment for the inner input text */
  align?: "center" | "right" | "left";
  /** @description whether to disable scientific notation input (e.g. 'e', 'E') */
  disabledScientific?: boolean;
}
```

## Events

```
export type GInputNumberEvents = {
  change: [cur: number | undefined, prev: number | undefined];
  input: [val: number | null | undefined];

  focus: [e: FocusEvent];
}

  "increase-icon": {};

GInputTag

form-inputtag · ./stories/input-tag.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GInputTag } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Models

Two-way bindings. Bind each one with the `v-model` syntax shown next to it — do not pass the prop and listen to its `update:` event separately.

```
export type GInputTagModels = {
  /**
   * @description binding value (array of string tags)
   * @default [] as string[]
   */
  modelValue?: string[]; // v-model="..."
}
```

## Props

```
export type GInputTagProps = {
  /**
   * @description help text for input tag
   * @default undefined
   */
  helpText?: string;
  /**
   * @description tag type
   * @default 'info'
   */
  tagType?: string;
  /**
   * @description tag effect
   * @default 'light'
   */
  tagEffect?: string;
  /**
   * @description tooltip theme, built-in theme: `dark` / `light`
   * @default 'light'
   */
  effect?: PopperEffect;
  /**
   * @description the key to trigger input tag
   * @default EVENT_CODE.enter
   */
  trigger?: "Enter" | "Space";
  /**
   * @description add a tag when a delimiter is matched
   * @default ''
   */
  delimiter?: string | RegExp;
  /**
   * @description input box size
   * @default 'default'
   */
  size?: string;
  /**
   * @description custom clear icon (DS `IconString`)
   * @default 'regular circle-xmark'
   */
  clearIcon?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description whether to disable input-tag
   * @default undefined
   */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description whether to trigger form validation
   * @default true
   */
  validateEvent?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description same as `id` in native input
   * @default undefined
   */
  id?: string;
  /**
   * @description same as `tabindex` in native input
   * @default 0
   */
  tabindex?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description native input autocomplete
   * @default 'off'
   */
  autocomplete?: string;
  /**
   * @description whether to save the input value when the input loses focus
   * @default true
   */
  saveOnBlur?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description the max tags number to be shown. Requires `collapse-tags`
   * @default 1
   */
  maxCollapseTags?: number;
  /** @description whether tags can be dragged */
  draggable?: boolean;
  /** @description whether to show clear button */
  clearable?: boolean;
  /** @description native input readonly */
  readonly?: boolean;
  /** @description native input autofocus */
  autofocus?: boolean;
  /** @description whether to collapse tags to a text */
  collapseTags?: boolean;
  /** @description whether to show all selected tags when hovering collapsed tag. Requires `collapse-tags` */
  collapseTagsTooltip?: boolean;
  /** @description same as `maxlength` in native input */
  maxlength?: string | number;
  /** @description same as `minlength` in native input */
  minlength?: string | number;
  /** @description max number of tags that can be entered */
  max?: number;
  /** @description placeholder of input */
  placeholder?: string;
  /** @description native `aria-label` attribute */
  ariaLabel?: string;
}
```

## Events

```
export type GInputTagEvents = {
  "update:modelValue": [value?: string | string[] | undefined];
  change: [value?: string | string[] | undefined];
  input: [value: string];
  "add-tag": [value: string | string[]];
  "remove-tag": [value: string, index: number];
  "drag-tag": [oldIndex: number, newIndex: number, value: string];
  focus: [evt: FocusEvent];
  blur: [evt: FocusEvent];
  clear: [];
}
```

## Slots

Each slot is typed with the props it passes to its content.

```
export type GInputTagSlots = {
  prefix: {};
  tag: { value: string; index: number; };
  suffix: {};
  helpText: { error: string | undefined; isError: boolean; };
}
```

## Exposed

Available on the component instance through a template ref.

```
export type GInputTagExposed = {
  focus: () => void;
  blur: () => void;
}
```

API description vue3 
## Props

```
export type LinkProps = {
  /**
   * @description type of link
   * @default 'primary'
   */
  type?: string;
  /**
   * @description size of link
   * @default 'md'
   */
  size?: string;
  /**
   * @description whether the component always shows an underline
   * @default false
   */
  underline?: boolean;
  /** @description whether the component is disabled */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description same as native hyperlink's `href`
   * @default ''
   */
  href?: string;
  /**
   * @description same as native hyperlink's `target`
   * @default '_self'
   */
  target?: string;
  /**
   * @description left icon name for the link
   * @default ''
   */
  iconLeft?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description right icon name for the link
   * @default ''
   */
  iconRight?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description status of the link, controls contextual appearance
  - default: Standalone link with standard styling
  - inline: Link designed to be embedded within text paragraphs

  Note: Use inline status for links that appear within paragraphs of text
   * @default 'default'
   */
  status?: string;
}
```

Todos los logos story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { LOGO_GROUPS, LOGO_NAMES, LOGO_SIZES } from "@flash-global66/g-logo/index.ts";
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GLogo } from '@flash-global66/g-logo/index.ts';
import { GSegmented } from '@flash-global66/g-segmented';

const NATURAL_SIZE = '__natural__';
const selectedSize = ref(NATURAL_SIZE);
const sizeOptions = [
  { label: 'Original', value: NATURAL_SIZE },
  ...Object.keys(LOGO_SIZES).map(s => ({
    label: s.toUpperCase(),
    value: s,
  })),
];
const isOnDark = (name: string) => name.endsWith('-on-dark');
const logoSizeProps = (sizeKey: string) =>
  sizeKey === NATURAL_SIZE ? {} : { size: sizeKey };
</script>

  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="space-y-10">
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap">
        <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 m-0">
          {{ LOGO_NAMES.length }} logos · colores originales del SVG
        </p>
        <g-segmented v-model="selectedSize" :options="sizeOptions" />
      </div>
      <section
        v-for="group in LOGO_GROUPS"
        :key="group.id"
        class="space-y-4"
      >
        <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-gray-800 m-0">{{ group.label }}</h3>
        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-6 items-center">
          <div
            v-for="name in group.names"
            :key="name"
            class="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 p-4 border rounded-md min-w-[120px]"
            :class="isOnDark(name) ? 'bg-gray-900 border-gray-700' : 'bg-white'"
          >
            <g-logo
              :name="name"
              v-bind="logoSizeProps(selectedSize)"
              :lazy-load="false"
            />
            <span
              class="text-xs font-mono text-center break-all"
              :class="isOnDark(name) ? 'text-gray-400' : 'text-gray-500'"
            >{{ name }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

GMenu

feedback-menu · ./stories/menu.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GMenu } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Props

```
export type MenuProps = {
  /** whether the menu is collapsed */
  collapse?: boolean;
  /** whether only one sub-menu can be active */
  uniqueOpened?: boolean;
  /**
   * whether vue-router mode is activated. If true, index will be used as
   * 'path' to activate the route action. Use with default-active to set the
   * active item on load.
   */
  router?: boolean;
  /**
   * layout mode of the menu
   *
   * @default 'vertical'
   */
  mode?: "horizontal" | "vertical";
  /**
   * index of active menu on page load
   *
   * @default ''
   */
  defaultActive?: string;
  /**
   * array that contains indexes of currently active sub-menus
   *
   * @default []
   */
  defaultOpeneds?: string[];
  /**
   * how sub-menus are triggered, only relevant in `horizontal` mode or when
   * `collapse` is `true`
   *
   * @default 'hover'
   */
  menuTrigger?: "click" | "hover";
  /**
   * whether to enable the collapse transition
   *
   * @deprecated no-op — see file-level comment for rationale
   * @default true
   */
  collapseTransition?: boolean;
  /**
   * offset of the appended sub menu popper
   *
   * @default 6
   */
  popperOffset?: number;
  /**
   * Tooltip theme of sub menu popper, effective for `mode="horizontal"`
   *
   * @default 'dark'
   */
  popperEffect?: string;
  /**
   * timeout in milliseconds to show sub menus
   *
   * @default 300
   */
  showTimeout?: number;
  /**
   * timeout in milliseconds to hide sub menus
   *
   * @default 300
   */
  hideTimeout?: number;
  /**
   * when the sub menu popper is inactive and `persistent` is `false`, it
   * will be destroyed
   *
   * @default true
   */
  persistent?: boolean;
  /**
   * background color of Menu (hex format) — CSS var only, no dynamic
   * hover-darken (see file-level comment)
   */
  backgroundColor?: string;
  /** text color of Menu (hex format) */
  textColor?: string;
  /** text color of currently active menu item (hex format) */
  activeTextColor?: string;
  /** custom class name for all popper menus */
  popperClass?: string;
}
```

## Events

```
export type MenuEvents = {
  open: [index: string, indexPath: string[]];
  close: [index: string, indexPath: string[]];
  select: [index: string, indexPath: string[], item: { index: string; indexPath: string[]; route?: unknown; }, routerResult?: Promise<unknown> | undefined];
}
```

  default: {};

  activeIndex: string;
  openedMenus: string[];
}

export type GPaginationProps = {

  pageCount?: number;
  /** @description current page number */

}

GPopover

feedback-popover · ./stories/popover.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GPopover } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Models

Two-way bindings. Bind each one with the `v-model` syntax shown next to it — do not pass the prop and listen to its `update:` event separately.

```
export type PopoverModels = {
  /**
   * whether popover is visible <br />
   * `visible / v-model:visible`
   */
  visible?: boolean | null; // v-model:visible="..."
}
```

## Props

```
export type PopoverProps = {
  /**
   * popover offset
   *
   * @default undefined
   */
  offset?: number;
  /** popover content, can be replaced with a default slot */
  content?: string;
  /** whether Popover is disabled */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /**
   * when popover inactive and persistent is false , popover will be destroyed
   *
   * @default true
   */
  persistent?: boolean;
  /**
   * whether a tooltip arrow is displayed or not.
   *
   * @default true
   */
  showArrow?: boolean;
  /**
   * how the popover is triggered
   * `click/hover/contextmenu`
   */
  trigger?: Arrayable<TooltipTriggerType>;
  /**
   * popover transition animation.
   *
   * @default 'gui-fade-in-linear'
   */
  transition?: string;
  /** whether popover dropdown is teleported to the body */
  teleported?: boolean;
  /**
   * popper theme, `light/dark`
   *
   * @default 'light'
   */
  effect?: PopperEffect;
  /**
   * whether the popover content is enterable (mouse can move from
   * reference into the content without closing a `hover`-triggered popover)
   *
   * @default true
   */
  enterable?: boolean;
  /**
   * popover placement <br />
   * `top/top-start/top-end` <br />
   * `bottom/bottom-start/bottom-end` <br />
   * `left/left-start/left-end` <br />
   * `right/right-start/right-end`
   */
  placement?: Placement;
  /**
   * parameters for
   * <a href="https://popper.js.org/docs/v2/" target="_blank">popper.js</a>
   */
  popperOptions?: Partial<Options>;
  /**
   * delay of appearance, in millisecond
   *
   * @default 0
   */
  showAfter?: number;
  /**
   * delay of disappear, in millisecond
   *
   * @default 200
   */
  hideAfter?: number;
  /**
   * timeout in milliseconds to hide tooltip
   *
   * @default 0
   */
  autoClose?: number;
  /**
   * <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Global_attributes/tabindex" target="_blank">
   *  tabindex
   * </a> of Popover
   */
  tabindex?: string | number;
  /**
   * popover width
   *
   * @default 150
   */
  width?: string | number;
  /** popover title */
  title?: string;
  /** custom class name for popover */
  popperClass?: ClassType;
  /** custom style popper */
  popperStyle?: StyleValue;
}
```

## Events

```
export type PopoverEvents = {
  /** triggers when popover shows */
  show: any[];
  /** triggers when the entering transition before */
  "before-enter": any[];
  /** triggers when the entering transition ends */
  "after-enter": any[];
  /** triggers when popover hides */
  hide: any[];
  /** triggers when the leaving transition before */
  "before-leave": any[];
  /** triggers when the leaving transition ends */
  "after-leave": any[];
  /**
   * triggers when the internal visible state settles after closing,
   * keeps `v-model:visible` in sync even when the close was not
   * initiated by the consumer (auto-close, click outside, escape, etc.)
   */
  "update:visible": any[];
}
```

## Slots

Each slot is typed with the props it passes to its content.

```
export type PopoverSlots = {
  /** default text content of popover */
  default: {};
  /** reference HTML element that triggers popover */
  reference: {};
}
```

GProgress

data-progress · ./stories/progress.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GProgress } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Props

```
export type GProgressProps = {
  /**
   * @description type of progress bar
   * @default 'line'
   */
  type?: string;
  /**
   * @description percentage, required
   * @default 0
   */
  percentage?: number;
  /**
   * @description the current status of progress bar
   * @default 'primary'
   */
  status?: string;
  /**
   * @description control the animation duration of indeterminate progress or striped flow progress
   * @default 3
   */
  duration?: number;
  /**
   * @description the width of progress bar
   * @default 6
   */
  strokeWidth?: number;
  /**
   * @description butt/circle/dashboard type shape at the end path
   * @default 'round'
   */
  strokeLinecap?: NonNullable<"butt" | "round" | "square" | "inherit"> | undefined;
  /**
   * @description the canvas width of circle progress bar
   * @default 126
   */
  width?: number;
  /**
   * @description whether to show percentage
   * @default true
   */
  showText?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description should show the loading spinner icon. Overrides status icon.
   * @default false
   */
  loading?: boolean;
  /** @default `${percentage}%` */
  format?: ProgressFn;
  /** @description set indeterminate progress */
  indeterminate?: boolean;
  /** @description whether to place the percentage inside progress bar, only works when `type` is 'line' */
  textInside?: boolean;
}
```

## Slots

Each slot is typed with the props it passes to its content.

```
export type GProgressSlots = {
  default: { percentage: number; };
}
```

<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GRadioGroup } from '../components/radio';

const selectedValue = ref('Value B');
const options = [
  { value: 'Value A', label: 'Importaciones' },
  { value: 'Value B', label: 'Inversiones' },
  { value: 'Value C', label: 'Exportaciones' },
  { value: 'Value D', label: 'Transferencias', disabled: true },
  { value: 'Value E', label: 'Otros' },
];
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <g-radio-group 
      ariaLabel="Grupo de opciones exclusivas" :disabled="false" id="custom-radio-group" layout="horizontal" name="radio-group" validateEvent
      v-model="selectedValue"
      :options="options"
    />
    <div class="text-3 text-primary-txt font-medium mt-5">
      Valor seleccionado: {{ selectedValue }}
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

API description vue3 
## Props

```
export type GScrollbarProps = {
  /**
   * @description height of scrollbar
   * @default ''
   */
  height?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description max height of scrollbar
   * @default ''
   */
  maxHeight?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description whether to use the native scrollbar
   * @default false
   */
  native?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description style of wrap
   * @default ''
   */
  wrapStyle?: StyleValue;
  /**
   * @description class of wrap
   * @default ''
   */
  wrapClass?: string | unknown[];
  /**
   * @description class of view
   * @default ''
   */
  viewClass?: string | unknown[];
  /**
   * @description style of view
   * @default ''
   */
  viewStyle?: string | unknown[] | Record<string, any>;
  /**
   * @description element tag of the view
   * @default 'div'
   */
  tag?: string;
  /**
   * @description minimum size of scrollbar
   * @default 20
   */
  minSize?: number;
  /**
   * @description Wrap tabindex
   * @default undefined
   */
  tabindex?: string | number;
  /** @description do not respond to container size changes, if the container size does not change, it is better to set it to optimize performance */
  noresize?: boolean;
  /** @description always show */
  always?: boolean;
  ariaOrientation?: string;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  /** @description id of view */
  id?: string;
  /** @description role of view */
  role?: string;
}
```

## Events

```
export type GScrollbarEvents = {

}

  default: {};
}
```

## Exposed

Available on the component instance through a template ref.

```
export type GScrollbarExposed = {
  /** @description scrollbar wrap ref */
  wrapRef: any;
  /** @description update scrollbar state manually */
  update: () => void;
  /** @description scrolls to a particular set of coordinates */
  scrollTo: { (xCord: number, yCord?: number | undefined): void; (options: ScrollToOptions): void; };
  /** @description set distance to scroll top */
  setScrollTop: (value: number) => void;
  /** @description set distance to scroll left */
  setScrollLeft: (value: number) => void;
  /** @description handle scroll event */
  handleScroll: () => void;
}
```

Uso de debounce story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { action } from "storybook/actions";
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '@flash-global66/g-config-provider/index.ts';
import { GSearchInput } from '@flash-global66/g-search-input/index.ts';

const search = ref('');
const lastSearch = ref('');
const searchCount = ref(0);
const handleSearch = (query: string) => {
  lastSearch.value = query;
  searchCount.value++;
  action('search')(query);
};
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="space-y-6">
      <div class="bg-gray-50 rounded-md p-4">
        <h3 class="text-base font-semibold mb-2">Debounce en acción</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-4">
          El debounce espera hasta que el usuario deje de escribir antes de emitir el evento de búsqueda.
          En este ejemplo, se configura un tiempo de espera de <strong>1000ms (1 segundo)</strong>.
        </p>

        <g-search-input
          v-model="search"
          placeholder="Escribe para buscar..."
          :debounce-time="1000"
          @search="handleSearch"
        />

        <div class="mt-4 p-3 bg-white rounded border text-sm">
          <div class="mb-2">
            <span class="font-medium">Valor actual:</span> 
            <span class="font-mono bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">{{ search }}</span>
            <span class="text-gray-400 ml-2">(se actualiza en tiempo real)</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span class="font-medium">Última búsqueda:</span> 
            <span class="font-mono bg-gray-100 px-1 rounded">{{ lastSearch }}</span>
            <span class="text-gray-400 ml-2">(se actualiza después del debounce o al presionar Enter)</span>
          </div>
          <div class="mt-2 text-gray-500">
            Número de búsquedas realizadas: {{ searchCount }}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="bg-gray-50 rounded-md p-4">
        <h3 class="text-base font-semibold mb-2">¿Cómo funciona el debounce?</h3>
        <ol class="list-decimal pl-5 space-y-2 text-sm">
          <li>Cuando escribes en el campo de búsqueda, el componente actualiza el v-model inmediatamente</li>
          <li>El componente espera el tiempo configurado (debounceTime) antes de emitir el evento <code>search</code></li>
          <li>Si sigues escribiendo dentro de ese tiempo, el temporizador se reinicia</li>
          <li>Solo cuando dejas de escribir por el tiempo configurado, o presionas Enter, se emite el evento</li>
          <li>Esto reduce significativamente el número de búsquedas y mejora el rendimiento</li>
        </ol>
      </div>
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

## Props

```
export type GSelectProps = {
  /**
   * @description configuration options, see the following table
   * @default defaultProps
   */
  props?: Props;
  /** @description whether Select is loading data from server */
  loading?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description label text
   * @default undefined
   */
  label: string;
  /** @description is multiple */
  multiple?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description autocomplete of select input
   * @default 'none'
   */
  autocomplete?: "none" | "both" | "list" | "inline";
  /**
   * @description custom clear icon
   * @default 'regular circle-xmark'
   */
  clearIcon?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description tooltip theme, built-in theme: `dark` / `light`
   * @default 'light'
   */
  effect?: PopperEffect;
  /**
   * @description The max tags number to be shown. To use this, `collapse-tags` must be true
   * @default 1
   */
  maxCollapseTags?: number;
  /**
   * @description
   * @default undefined
   */
  estimatedOptionHeight?: number;
  /**
   * @description placeholder for the internal search field shown when `searchable` is true
   * @default 'Buscar'
   */
  searchPlaceholder?: string;
  /**
   * @description The height of the dropdown panel, 34px for each item
   * @default 274
   */
  height?: number;
  /**
   * @description The height of the dropdown item
   * @default 55
   */
  itemHeight?: number;
  /**
   * @description Maximum number of lines for item title (0 = unlimited)
   * @default 1
   */
  titleLines?: number;
  /**
   * @description Maximum number of lines for item description (0 = unlimited)
   * @default 0
   */
  descriptionLines?: number;
  /**
   * @description maximum number of options user can select when multiple is true. No limit when set to 0
   * @default 0
   */
  multipleLimit?: number;
  /**
   * @description whether reserve the keyword after select filtered option.
   * @default true
   */
  reserveKeyword?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description data of the options, the key of `value` and `label` can be customize by `props`
   * @default []
   */
  options: OptionType[];
  /**
   * help text for input
   *
   * @default undefined
   */
  helpText?: string;
  /**
   * @description when select dropdown is inactive and `persistent` is `false`, select dropdown will be destroyed
   * @default true
   */
  persistent?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description custom class name for Select's dropdown
   * @default ''
   */
  popperClass?: string;
  /**
   * @description [popper.js](https://popper.js.org/docs/v2/) parameters
   * @default ({}) as Partial<Options>
   */
  popperOptions?: Partial<Options>;
  /**
   * @description position of dropdown
   * @default 'bottom-start'
   */
  placement?: Placement;
  /**
   * @description unique identity key name for value, required when value is an object
   * @default 'value'
   */
  valueKey?: string;
  /**
   * @description whether to trigger form validation
   * @default true
   */
  validateEvent?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description offset of the dropdown
   * @default 12
   */
  offset?: number;
  /**
   * @description Determines whether the arrow is displayed
   * @default true
   */
  showArrow?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description list of possible positions for dropdown
   * @default ['bottom-start', 'top-start', 'right', 'left']
   */
  fallbackPlacements?: Placement[];
  /**
   * @description tag type
   * @default 'info'
   */
  tagType?: string;
  /**
   * @description tag effect
   * @default 'light'
   */
  tagEffect?: string;
  /**
   * @description tabindex for input
   * @default 0
   */
  tabindex?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description message error
   * @default undefined
   */
  messageError?: string;
  /**
   * @description prefix icon
   * @default ''
   */
  prefixIcon?: IconString;
  /**
   * @description if it is `true`, the width of the dropdown panel is the same as the input box.
  if it is `false`, the width is automatically calculated based on the value of `label`,
  or it can be set to a number to make it a fixed width
   * @default true
   */
  fitInputWidth?: number | boolean;
  /**
   * @description valor retornado al limpiar; para usar `undefined`, pasar `() => undefined`
   * @default undefined
   */
  valueOnClear?: string | number | boolean | Function;
  /** @description whether creating new items is allowed. To use this, `filterable` must be true */
  allowCreate?: boolean;
  /** @description for non-filterable Select, this prop decides if the option menu pops up when the input is focused */
  automaticDropdown?: boolean;
  /** @description whether select can be cleared */
  clearable?: boolean;
  /** @description whether to collapse tags to a text when multiple selecting */
  collapseTags?: boolean;
  /** @description whether show all selected tags when mouse hover text of collapse-tags. To use this, `collapse-tags` must be true */
  collapseTagsTooltip?: boolean;
  /** @description */
  defaultFirstOption?: boolean;
  /** @description is disabled */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** @description is filterable */
  filterable?: boolean;
  /** @description shows a search field inside the dropdown panel (independent from `filterable`,
  which shows the search field in the closed trigger instead). Matches against both title and
  description of each option. */
  searchable?: boolean;
  /** @description removes the trigger's border across all states (default, hover, focused,
  complete), for embedding the select inside containers that already provide their own
  border/outline treatment */
  borderless?: boolean;
  /** @description whether select dropdown is teleported, if `true` it will be teleported to where `append-to` sets */
  teleported?: boolean;
  /** @description whether search data from server */
  remote?: boolean;
  /** @description Controls whether the scrollbar is always displayed */
  scrollbarAlwaysOn?: boolean;
  /** @description when true (and multiple), selected items are not shown as tags; the select looks empty but keeps the value */
  emptyDisplay?: boolean;
  /** @description the name attribute of select input */
  name?: string;
  /** @description placeholder, the default is 'Please select' */
  placeholder?: string;
  /** @description valores vacíos soportados por el componente */
  emptyValues?: unknown[];
  ariaLabel?: string;
  /** @description */
  filterMethod?: Function;
  /** @description */
  id?: string;
  /** @description displayed text while loading data from server, default is 'Loading' */
  loadingText?: string;
  /** @description displayed text when there is no options, you can also use slot empty, the default is 'No Data' */
  noDataText?: string;
  /** @description displayed text when no data matches the filtering query, you can also use slot `empty`, default is 'No matching data' */
  noMatchText?: string;
  /** @description function that gets called when the input value changes. Its parameter is the current input value. To use this, `filterable` must be true */
  remoteMethod?: Function;
  /** @description which element the select dropdown appends to */
  appendTo?: string;
}
```

## Events

```
export type GSelectEvents = {
  blur: [evt: FocusEvent];
  focus: [evt: FocusEvent];
  clear: [];
  "update:modelValue": [val: any];
  change: [val: any];
  "remove-tag": [val: unknown];
  "visible-change": [visible: boolean];
}
```

<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GSelect } from '@flash-global66/g-select/index.ts';

const options = [
  { value: '1', title: 'Opción 1' },
  { value: '2', title: 'Opción 2' },
  { value: '3', title: 'Opción 3' },
  { value: '4', title: 'Opción 4' },
  { value: '5', title: 'Opción 5' },
  { value: '6', title: 'Opción 6' },
];

const value = ref('');
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <g-select label="Etiqueta" :options="options" placeholder="Placeholder" v-model="value" />
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>
Personalizado de opciones con props story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GSelect } from '@flash-global66/g-select/index.ts';

const initials = ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e', 'f', 'g', 'h', 'i', 'j'];
const value = ref();
const value2 = ref();
const options = Array.from({ length: 1000 }).map((_, idx) => ({
  value: `Option ${idx + 1}`,
  title: `${initials[idx % 10]}${idx} - option ${idx + 1}`,
  description: `Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit. Mauris in enim elementum, sagittis velit eu, feugiat sem. Sed lacinia tincidunt lacinia.`,
  icon: idx % 2 === 0 ? 'regular bolt' : 'regular user',
  disabled: idx % 3 === 0 && idx !== 0,
}));
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="flex flex-row gap-4 items-center">
      <g-select
        v-model="value"
        :options="options"
        placeholder="Please select"
        :item-height="90"
        :filterable="true"
        prefix-icon="regular bolt"
        label="Label"
        style="width: 300px"
        :clearable="true"
        helpText="This is a help text"
        :description-lines="2"
      />
      <g-select
        v-model="value2"
        :options="options"
        placeholder="Please select"
        :item-height="90"
        :filterable="true"
        prefix-icon="regular bolt"
        label="Label"
        style="width: 300px"
        :clearable="true"
        helpText="This is a help text"
        :fit-input-width="440"
        :description-lines="2"
      />
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

Selección múltiple story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GSelect } from '@flash-global66/g-select/index.ts';

const value = ref<string[]>([]);
const options = [
  { value: 'react', title: 'React' },
  { value: 'vue', title: 'Vue' },
  { value: 'angular', title: 'Angular' },
  { value: 'svelte', title: 'Svelte' },
  { value: 'solid', title: 'Solid' },
];
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-6">
      <g-select
        v-model="value"
        label="Frameworks (múltiple)"
        placeholder="Elige uno o más"
        :options="options"
        multiple
        :filterable="true"
        :clearable="true"
        style="width: 320px"
        help-text="Selecciona varios frameworks. Cada valor aparece como tag y puedes quitarlo con la X."
      />
      <p class="text-3 text-secondary-txt">Seleccionados: {{ value?.length ? value.join(', ') : 'ninguno' }}</p>
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>
Selección múltiple (estado empty) story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GSelect } from '@flash-global66/g-select/index.ts';

const value = ref<string[]>(['react', 'vue']);
const options = [
  { value: 'react', title: 'React' },
  { value: 'vue', title: 'Vue' },
  { value: 'angular', title: 'Angular' },

];

        multiple
        empty-display
        :filterable="true"
        :clearable="true"
        style="width: 320px"
        help-text="Los seleccionados no se muestran como tags (valor: React, Vue)."

GSkeleton

data-skeleton · ./stories/skeleton.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GSkeleton } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Props

```
export type SkeletonProps = {
  /**
   * whether showing the animation
   *
   * @default false
   */
  animated?: boolean;
  /**
   * how many fake items to render to the DOM
   *
   * @default 1
   */
  count?: number;
  /**
   * numbers of the row, only useful when no template slot were given
   *
   * @default 3
   */
  rows?: number;
  /**
   * displays the real content (`default` slot) or the loading skeleton
   *
   * @default true
   */
  loading?: boolean;
  /**
   * Rendering delay in milliseconds, or `{ leading, trailing, initVal }`
   * to control the leading/trailing edges independently
   */
  throttle?: number | ThrottleConfig;
}
```

## Slots

Each slot is typed with the props it passes to its content.

```
export type SkeletonSlots = {
  /** Custom rendering skeleton template */
  template: {};
  /** Real rendering DOM */
  default: {};
}
```

## Exposed

Available on the component instance through a template ref.

```
export type SkeletonExposed = {
  ns: NamespaceHelpers;
  uiLoading: boolean;
}
```

GSwitch

form-switch · ./stories/switch.stories.ts
Info
No description found. Write a jsdoc comment such as /** Component description */.
Info
Specify an @import jsdoc tag on your component or your stories meta such as @import import { GSwitch } from 'my-design-system';
API description vue3 
## Models

Two-way bindings. Bind each one with the `v-model` syntax shown next to it — do not pass the prop and listen to its `update:` event separately.

```
export type GSwitchModels = {
  /**
   * @description binding value, it should be equivalent to either `active-value` or `inactive-value`, by default it's `boolean` type
   * @default false
   */
  modelValue?: string | number | boolean; // v-model="..."
}
```

## Props

```
export type GSwitchProps = {
  /** @description whether Switch is disabled */
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** @description whether Switch is in loading state */
  loading?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description width of Switch
   * @default ''
   */
  width?: string | number;
  /** @description whether icon or text is displayed inside dot, only the first character will be rendered for text */
  inlinePrompt?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description text displayed when in `on` state
   * @default ''
   */
  activeText?: string;
  /**
   * @description text displayed when in `off` state
   * @default ''
   */
  inactiveText?: string;
  /**
   * @description switch value when in `on` state
   * @default true
   */
  activeValue?: string | number | boolean;
  /**
   * @description switch value when in `off` state
   * @default false
   */
  inactiveValue?: string | number | boolean;
  /**
   * @description input name of Switch
   * @default ''
   */
  name?: string;
  /**
   * @description whether to trigger form validation
   * @default true
   */
  validateEvent?: boolean;
  /** @description size of Switch */
  size?: ComponentSize;
  /** @description icon name displayed in action when in `off` state */
  inactiveActionIcon?: IconString;
  /** @description icon name displayed in action when in `on` state */
  activeActionIcon?: IconString;
  /** @description icon name displayed when in `on` state, overrides `active-text` */
  activeIcon?: IconString;
  /** @description icon name displayed when in `off` state, overrides `inactive-text` */
  inactiveIcon?: IconString;
  /** @description before-change hook before the switch state changes. If `false` is returned or a `Promise` is returned and then is rejected, will stop switching */
  beforeChange?: (() => boolean | Promise<boolean>);
  /** @description id for input */
  id?: string;
  /** @description tabindex for input */
  tabindex?: string | number;
  ariaLabel?: string;
}
```

## Events

```
export type GSwitchEvents = {
  "update:modelValue": [val: string | number | boolean];
  change: [val: string | number | boolean];
  input: [val: string | number | boolean];
}
```

## Slots

Each slot is typed with the props it passes to its content.

```
export type GSwitchSlots = {
  "active-action": {};
  "inactive-action": {};
}
```

## Exposed

Available on the component instance through a template ref.

```
export type GSwitchExposed = {
  /** @description manual focus to the switch component */
  focus: () => void;
  /** @description whether Switch is checked */
  checked: boolean;
}
```

          v-model="value3"
          width="140px"
          active-text="Habilitado"
          inactive-text="Deshabilitado"
          inline-prompt
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>

## Props

```
export type GTableProps = {
  /** @description whether Table has vertical border */
  border?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description whether to allow drag the last column
   * @default true
   */
  allowDragLastColumn?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description table data
   * @default []
   */
  data?: any[];
  /** @default {} */
  style?: CSSProperties;
  /** @default '' */
  className?: string;
  /**
   * @description sets the algorithm used to lay out table cells, rows, and columns
   * @default 'fixed'
   */
  tableLayout?: Layout;
  /** @description whether Table is striped */
  stripe?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description configuration for rendering nested data
   * @default () => {
      return {
          hasChildren: 'hasChildren',
          children: 'children',
          checkStrictly: false,
      };
  }
   */
  treeProps?: TreeProps;
  /**
   * @description whether width of column automatically fits its container
   * @default true
   */
  fit?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description whether Table header is visible
   * @default true
   */
  showHeader?: boolean;
  /** @description whether to display a summary row */
  showSummary?: boolean;
  /** @description whether current row is highlighted */
  highlightCurrentRow?: boolean;
  /** @description whether expand all rows by default, works when the table has a column type="expand" or contains tree structure data */
  defaultExpandAll?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description controls the behavior of master checkbox in multi-select tables when only some rows are selected (but not all). If true, all rows will be selected, else deselected
   * @default true
   */
  selectOnIndeterminate?: boolean;
  /**
   * @description horizontal indentation of tree data
   * @default 16
   */
  indent?: number;
  /** @description whether to lazy loading data */
  lazy?: boolean;
  /** @description always show scrollbar */
  scrollbarAlwaysOn?: boolean;
  /** @description ensure main axis minimum-size doesn't follow the content */
  flexible?: boolean;
  /** @default undefined */
  scrollbarTabindex?: string | number;
  /**
   * @description whether to preserve expanded row content in DOM when collapsed
   * @default false
   */
  preserveExpandedContent?: boolean;
  /** @description set the default sort column and order. property `prop` is used to set default sort column, property `order` is used to set default sort order */
  defaultSort?: Sort;
  appendFilterPanelTo?: string;
  /** @description the options for the overflow tooltip, [see the following tooltip component](tooltip.html#attributes) */
  tooltipOptions?: Partial<Pick<GTooltipProps, "showArrow" | "appendTo" | "transition" | "effect" | "enterable" | "popperClass" | "offset" | "placement" | "popperOptions" | "showAfter" | "hideAfter">>;
  /** @description function that returns custom class names for a row, or a string assigning class names for every row */
  rowClassName?: ColumnCls<any>;
  /** @description function that returns custom style for a row, or an object assigning custom style for every row */
  rowStyle?: ColumnStyle<any>;
  /** @description the `effect` of the overflow tooltip */
  tooltipEffect?: string;
  /** @description custom summary method */
  summaryMethod?: SummaryMethod<any>;
  /** @description displayed text for the first column of summary row */
  sumText?: string;
  /** @description table's height. By default it has an `auto` height. If its value is a number, the height is measured in pixels; if its value is a string, the value will be assigned to element's style.height, the height is affected by external styles */
  height?: string | number;
  /** @description table's max-height. The legal value is a number or the height in px */
  maxHeight?: string | number;
  onScroll?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  /** @description key of row data, used for optimizing rendering. Required if `reserve-selection` is on or display tree data. When its type is String, multi-level access is supported, e.g. `user.info.id`, but `user.info[0].id` is not supported, in which case `Function` should be used */
  rowKey?: string | ((row: any) => string);
  /** @description function that returns custom class names for a cell, or a string assigning class names for every cell */
  cellClassName?: CellCls<any>;
  /** @description function that returns custom style for a cell, or an object assigning custom style for every cell */
  cellStyle?: CellStyle<any>;
  /** @description function that returns custom class names for a row in table header, or a string assigning class names for every row in table header */
  headerRowClassName?: ColumnCls<any>;
  /** @description function that returns custom style for a row in table header, or an object assigning custom style for every row in table header */
  headerRowStyle?: ColumnStyle<any>;
  /** @description function that returns custom class names for a cell in table header, or a string assigning class names for every cell in table header */
  headerCellClassName?: CellCls<any>;
  /** @description function that returns custom style for a cell in table header, or an object assigning custom style for every cell in table header */
  headerCellStyle?: CellStyle<any>;
  /** @description set expanded rows by this prop, prop's value is the keys of expand rows, you should set row-key before using this prop */
  expandRowKeys?: string[];
  /** @description method that returns rowspan and colspan */
  spanMethod?: ((data: { row: any; rowIndex: number; column: TableColumnCtx<any>; columnIndex: number; }) => number[] | { rowspan: number; colspan: number; }) | undefined;
  /** @description method for loading child row data, only works when `lazy` is true */
  load?: ((row: any, treeNode: TreeNode, resolve: (data: any[]) => void) => void);
  /** @description whether to hide extra content and show them in a tooltip when hovering on the cell.It will affect all the table columns */
  showOverflowTooltip?: boolean | Partial<Pick<GTooltipProps, "showArrow" | "appendTo" | "transition" | "effect" | "enterable" | "popperClass" | "offset" | "placement" | "popperOptions" | "showAfter" | "hideAfter">>;
  /** @description function that formats cell tooltip content, works when `show-overflow-tooltip` is `true` */
  tooltipFormatter?: TableOverflowTooltipFormatter<any>;
  /** @description table width. By default it has an `auto` width. If its value is a number, the width is measured in pixels; if its value is a string, the value will be assigned to element's style.width, the width is affected by external styles */
  width?: string | number;
  /** @description minimum width (px) of the table for horizontal scroll; see TableProps.scrollMinWidth */
  scrollMinWidth?: string | number;
  /** @description key of current row, a set only prop */
  currentRowKey?: string | number;
  /** @description displayed text when data is empty. You can customize this area with `#empty` */
  emptyText?: string;
  onSelect?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onSelect-all'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onSelection-change'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-mouse-enter'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-mouse-leave'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-contextmenu'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-click'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-dblclick'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onRow-click'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onRow-contextmenu'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onRow-dblclick'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onHeader-click'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onHeader-contextmenu'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onSort-change'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onFilter-change'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCurrent-change'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onHeader-dragend'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onExpand-change'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-edit-open'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-edit-close'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-edit-change'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
  'onCell-edit-validate'?: ((...args: any[]) => any);
}
```

  resizeState: { width: number | null; height: number | null; headerHeight: number | null; };
  isGroup: boolean;
  bodyWidth: string;
  tableBodyStyles: { width: string; };
  emptyBlockStyle: { width: string; height: string; } | null;
  debouncedUpdateLayout: _.DebouncedFunc<() => void>;
  handleFixedMousewheel: (event: any, data: any) => void;
  /** @description used in single selection Table, set a certain row selected. If called without any parameter, it will clear selection */
  setCurrentRow: (row: any) => void;
  /** @description returns the currently selected rows */
  getSelectionRows: () => any[];
  /** @description used in multiple selection Table, toggle if a certain row is selected. With the second parameter, you can directly set if this row is selected */
  toggleRowSelection: (row: any, selected?: boolean | undefined, ignoreSelectable?: boolean) => void;
  /** @description used in multiple selection Table, clear user selection */
  clearSelection: () => void;
  /** @description clear filters of the columns whose `columnKey` are passed in. If no params, clear all filters */
  clearFilter: (columnKeys?: string[] | undefined) => void;
  /** @description used in multiple selection Table, toggle select all and deselect all */
  toggleAllSelection: () => void;
  /** @description used in expandable Table or tree Table, toggle if a certain row is expanded. With the second parameter, you can directly set if this row is expanded or collapsed */
  toggleRowExpansion: (row: any, expanded?: boolean | undefined) => void;
  /** @description clear sorting, restore data to the original order */
  clearSort: () => void;
  /** @description refresh the layout of Table. When the visibility of Table changes, you may need to call this method to get a correct layout */
  doLayout: () => void;
  /** @description sort Table manually. Property `prop` is used to set sort column, property `order` is used to set sort order */
  sort: (prop: string, order: string) => void;
  /** @description used in lazy Table, must set `rowKey`, update key children */
  updateKeyChildren: (key: string, data: any[]) => void;
  t: Translator;
  setDragVisible: (visible: boolean) => void;
  context: Table<any>;
  computedSumText: any;
  computedEmptyText: any;
  scrollbarViewStyle: { display: string; verticalAlign: string; };
  scrollbarStyle: { height: string; maxHeight?: undefined; } | { maxHeight: string; height?: undefined; } | { height?: undefined; maxHeight?: undefined; };
  scrollBarRef: any;
  /** @description scrolls to a particular set of coordinates */
  scrollTo: (options: number | ScrollToOptions, yCoord?: number | undefined) => void;
  /** @description set horizontal scroll position */
  setScrollLeft: (left?: number | undefined) => void;
  /** @description set vertical scroll position */
  setScrollTop: (top?: number | undefined) => void;
}
```

<script lang="ts" setup>
import { GLink } from "@flash-global66/g-link";
import { h } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GTable } from '../components/table/index.ts';
import { GTableColumn } from '../components/table/index.ts';
import { GTag } from '@flash-global66/g-tag';

const tableData = [
  {
    address: 'Lohrbergstr. 86c, Süd Lilli, Saarland',
    tags: ['Office', 'Home', 'Park', 'Garden'],
    url: 'https://github.com/element-plus/element-plus/issues',
  },
  {
    address: '760 A Street, South Frankfield, Illinois',
    tags: ['error', 'warning', 'success', 'info'],
    url: 'https://github.com/element-plus/element-plus/pulls',
  },
  {
    address: 'Arnold-Ohletz-Str. 41a, Alt Malinascheid, Thüringen',
    tags: ['one', 'two', 'three', 'four', 'five'],
    url: 'https://github.com/element-plus/element-plus/discussions',
  },
  {
    address: '23618 Windsor Drive, West Ricardoview, Idaho',
    tags: ['blue', 'white', 'dark', 'gray', 'red', 'bright'],
    url: 'https://github.com/element-plus/element-plus/actions',
  },
];
const tableRowFormatter = (data: TableTooltipData<any>) => {
  return `${data.cellValue}: table formatter`;
};
const withVNode = (data: TableTooltipData<any>) => {
  return h(GLink, { type: 'primary', href: data.cellValue }, () =>
    h('span', null, data.cellValue),
  );
};
const tagsFormatter = (data: TableTooltipData<any>) => {
  return data.row.tags.join(', ');
};
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <g-table
      :data="tableData"
      show-overflow-tooltip
      :tooltip-formatter="tableRowFormatter"
      style="width: 100%"
      border
    >
      <g-table-column
        prop="address"
        label="extends table formatter"
        width="240"
      />
      <g-table-column
        prop="tags"
        label="formatter object"
        width="240"
        :tooltip-formatter="tagsFormatter"
      >
        <template #default="{ row }">
          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <g-tag
              v-for="tag in row.tags"
              :key="tag"
              type="info"
            >
              {{ tag }}
            </g-tag>
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
      <g-table-column
        prop="url"
        label="with vnode"
        width="240"
        :tooltip-formatter="withVNode"
      />
    </g-table>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";

import { GInput } from '@flash-global66/g-input';
import { GSelect } from '@flash-global66/g-select';
import { GTable } from '../components/table/index.ts';
import { GTableColumn } from '../components/table/index.ts';

const tableData = ref([
  {
    mode: 'send',
    currency: 'USD',
    amount: 1000,
    reason: 'initial-investment',
    detail:
      'Este es un detalle de la inversión inicial realizada en el proyecto.',
    isEditable: false,

    mode: 'receive',
    currency: 'EUR',
    amount: 500,
    reason: 'marketing-expenses',

      'Este es una descripción detallada de los gastos de marketing asociados con el proyecto.',

  },
  {

    amount: 3000,
    reason: 'miscellaneous-expenses',
    detail:
      'Este es una descripción detallada de los gastos misceláneos del proyecto.',
    isEditable: false,
  },
  {
    mode: 'send',
    currency: 'AUD',
    amount: 1200,
    reason: 'travel-expenses',
    detail:
      'Este es una descripción detallada de los gastos de viaje relacionados con el proyecto.',
    isEditable: false,
  },
  {
    mode: 'send',
    currency: 'CAD',
    amount: 800,
    reason: 'consulting-fees',
    detail:
      'Este es una descripción detallada de los honorarios de consultoría del proyecto.',
    isEditable: false,
  },
]);
const handleClick = () => {
  console.log('click');
};
const handleEdit = (index: number, row: any) => {
  tableData.value.splice(index, 1, {
    ...row,
    isEditable: !row.isEditable,
  });
};
const deleteRow = (index: number) => {
  tableData.value.splice(index, 1);
};
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <g-table :data="tableData" style="width: 100%" height="100%" border>
      <g-table-column prop="mode" label="Modalidad">
        <template #default="scope">
          <div v-if="scope.row.isEditable" class="py-1">
            <g-select
              v-model="scope.row.mode"
              :options="[
                { title: 'Send', value: 'send', icon: 'regular gift' },
                { title: 'Receive', value: 'receive', icon: 'regular money-bill-alt' }
              ]"
              @change="handleClick"
            />
          </div>
          <div v-else>
            {{ scope.row.mode }}
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
      <g-table-column prop="currency" label="Moneda">
        <template #default="scope">
          <div v-if="scope.row.isEditable" class="py-1">
            <g-select
              v-model="scope.row.currency"
              :options="[
                { title: 'USD', value: 'USD' },
                { title: 'EUR', value: 'EUR' },
                { title: 'GBP', value: 'GBP' },
                { title: 'JPY', value: 'JPY' },
                { title: 'AUD', value: 'AUD' },
                { title: 'CAD', value: 'CAD' }
              ]"
              @change="handleClick"
            />
          </div>
          <div v-else>
            {{ scope.row.currency }}
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
      <g-table-column prop="amount" label="Monto">
        <template #default="scope">
          <div v-if="scope.row.isEditable" class="py-1">
            <g-input
              v-model.number="scope.row.amount"
              type="number"
              @change="handleClick"
            >
              <template #suffix>
                <div class="flex gap-2 items-center">
                  <span class="text-gray-4">{{ scope.row.currency }}</span>
                </div>
              </template>
            </g-input>
          </div>
          <div v-else>
            {{ scope.row.amount }}
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
      <g-table-column prop="reason" label="Razón">
        <template #default="scope">
          <div v-if="scope.row.isEditable" class="py-1">
            <g-select
              v-model="scope.row.reason"
              :options="[
                { title: 'Inversión incial', value: 'initial-investment' },
                { title: 'Gastos de marketing', value: 'marketing-expenses' },
                { title: 'Costos operativos', value: 'operational-costs' },
                { title: 'Gastos misceláneos', value: 'miscellaneous-expenses' },
                { title: 'Gastos de viaje', value: 'travel-expenses' },
                { title: 'Honorarios de consultoría', value: 'consulting-fees' }
              ]"
              @change="handleClick"
              :fit-input-width="240"
            />
          </div>
          <div v-else>
            {{ scope.row.reason }}
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
      <g-table-column prop="detail" label="Detalle" width="300">
        <template #default="scope">
          <div v-if="scope.row.isEditable" class="py-1">
            <g-input
              v-model="scope.row.detail"
              type="text"
              label="Detalle"
              @change="handleClick"
            />
          </div>
          <div v-else>
            {{ scope.row.detail }}
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
      <g-table-column label="Acciones" align="center" width="100">
        <template #default="scope">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-center items gap-2">
            <g-icon-button
              v-if="!scope.row.isEditable"
              @click="handleEdit(scope.$index, scope.row)"
              variant="grey"
              icon="regular pen"
              size="small" 
            />
            <g-icon-button
              v-else
              @click="handleEdit(scope.$index, scope.row)"
              variant="grey"
              icon="regular check"
              size="small"
            />
            <g-icon-button
              variant="grey"
              icon="regular trash"
              size="small"
              @click="deleteRow(scope.$index)"
            />
          </div>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>
    </g-table>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

<script lang="ts" setup>
import { useTableCellSelect } from "../components/table/index.ts";
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GTable } from '../components/table/index.ts';
import { GTableColumn } from '../components/table/index.ts';

const tableData = ref([
  { id: 1, name: 'Empresa Alpha', purpose: 1 },
  { id: 2, name: 'Empresa Beta', purpose: 2 },
  { id: 3, name: 'Empresa Gamma', purpose: 3 },
]);
const purposeOptions = [
  {
    value: 1,
    title: 'Pago a proveedores',
    description:
      'Transferencias a proveedores nacionales o internacionales.',
  },
  {
    value: 2,
    title: 'Pago de servicios digitales y plataformas',
    description:
      'Incluye plataformas publicitarias, SaaS y otros servicios en línea.',
  },
  {
    value: 3,
    title: 'Otros conceptos',
    description:
      'Operaciones que no encajan en las categorías anteriores.',
  },
];
const { cellOptions: purposeCellOptions } = useTableCellSelect(
  tableData,
  {
    options: purposeOptions,
    label: 'Motivo',
    rowKey: 'id',
    expandColspan: 2,
    expandDirection: 'right',
  },
);
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <p class="text-3 text-secondary-txt mb-md max-w-xl">
      Columna «Motivo» estrecha (120px): en lectura solo el <strong>título</strong> de la opción (hasta 2 líneas con recorte); pasa el cursor para el texto completo vía <code class="text-2">title</code>. Al editar, en el menú cada opción muestra título + <code class="text-2">description</code> (la descripción, como máximo 2 líneas en el listado).
    </p>
    <g-table :data="tableData" style="width: 100%" :cell-style="{ zIndex: 'auto' }">
      <g-table-column prop="name" label="Nombre" width="200" />
      <g-table-column
        prop="purpose"
        label="Motivo"
        min-width="120"
        width="120"
        cell-type="select"
        :cell-options="purposeCellOptions"
        empty-action-text="Seleccionar"
      />
    </g-table>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

<script lang="ts" setup>
import { ref } from "vue";

import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';

import { GTableColumn } from '../components/table/index.ts';

  {
    name: 'Producto A',
    color: '#ff0000',
    customValue: 'Valor 1',
    isEditing: false,
  },
  {
    name: 'Producto B',
    color: '#00ff00',
    customValue: 'Valor 2',
    isEditing: false,
  },
  {
    name: 'Producto C',
    color: '#0000ff',
    customValue: 'Valor 3',
    isEditing: false,
  },
]);

  <g-config-provider>
    <g-table :data="tableData" border style="width: 100%" :cell-style="{ zIndex: 'auto' }">

      <g-table-column prop="name" label="Nombre" />

      <g-table-column prop="name" label="Nombre" />
      <g-table-column prop="name" label="Nombre" />

      <g-table-column prop="customValue" label="Custom Edit" width="200">
        <template #default="{ row, column, $index }">
          <g-cell-edit
            v-model="row.isEditing"
            :row="row"
            :column="column"
            :index="$index"
            prop="customValue"
            expand-direction="left"
            expand-colspan="1"
          >
            <template #view="{ toggle }">
              <div class="w-full flex items-center justify-between">
                <span>{{ row.customValue }}</span>
              </div>
            </template>

            <template #edit="{ close }">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2 w-full px-2">
                  <g-input v-model="row.customValue" size="small" @keydown.enter="close" @blur="close" autofocus class="w-full" />
                </div>
            </template>
          </g-cell-edit>
        </template>
      </g-table-column>

      <g-table-column prop="color" label="Otro Campo" />
    </g-table>
  </g-config-provider>

  {

    a: 'A2',
    b: 'B2',

    d: 'D2',

  },
  {
    id: 3,
    a: 'A3',
    b: 'B3',
    commentRight: 'Texto largo para ver overlay',
    d: 'D3',
    commentLeft: 'Overlay a la izquierda',
    f: 'F3',
    lastCol: 'Siempre left',
  },
]);
const { cellOptions: rightOptions } = useTableCellInput(tableData, {
  placeholder: 'Editar (right 3)...',
  useRowIndex: true,
  expandColspan: 3,
  expandDirection: 'right',
});
const { cellOptions: leftOptions } = useTableCellInput(tableData, {
  placeholder: 'Editar (left 2)...',
  useRowIndex: true,
  expandColspan: 2,
  expandDirection: 'left',
});
const { cellOptions: lastOptions } = useTableCellInput(tableData, {
  placeholder: 'Última col...',
  useRowIndex: true,
});
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <g-table :data="tableData" border style="width: 100%" :cell-style="{ zIndex: 'auto' }">
      <g-table-column prop="a" label="Col 1" width="90" />
      <g-table-column prop="b" label="Col 2" width="90" />
      <g-table-column
        prop="commentRight"
        label="Col 3 (right 3)"
        cell-type="input"
        expand-colspan="3"
        expand-direction="right"
        :cell-options="rightOptions"
      />
      <g-table-column prop="d" label="Col 4" width="90" />
      <g-table-column
        prop="commentLeft"
        label="Col 5 (left 2)"
        cell-type="input"
        expand-colspan="2"
        expand-direction="left"
        :cell-options="leftOptions"
      />
      <g-table-column prop="f" label="Col 6" width="90" />
      <g-table-column
        prop="lastCol"
        label="Col 7 (última)"
        cell-type="input"
        :cell-options="lastOptions"
      />
    </g-table>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>
Celda con validación (rules) story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { useTableCellInput } from "../components/table/index.ts";
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GButton } from '@flash-global66/g-button';
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GTable } from '../components/table/index.ts';
import { GTableColumn } from '../components/table/index.ts';

const tableData = ref([
  { id: 1, name: 'Juan Pérez', email: '', age: '', website: '' },
  {
    id: 2,
    name: 'María García',
    email: 'maria@example.com',
    age: '28',
    website: 'https://maria.dev',
  },
  {
    id: 3,
    name: 'Carlos López',
    email: 'carlos-invalid',
    age: '150',
    website: 'not-a-url',
  },
  { id: 4, name: 'Ana Martínez', email: '', age: '', website: '' },
]);
const { cellOptions: emailCellOptions, validation: emailValidation } =
  useTableCellInput(tableData, {
    label: 'Email',
    placeholder: 'correo@ejemplo.com',
    emptyActionText: 'Agregar email',
    rules: [
      {
        required: true,
        message: 'El email es requerido',
        trigger: 'blur',
      },
      {
        type: 'email',
        message: 'Ingresa un email válido',
        trigger: 'blur',
      },
    ],
    useRowIndex: true,
    onValidationFailed: (errors, row, prop, index) => {
      console.log('Email validación fallida:', {
        errors,
        row,
        prop,
        index,
      });
    },
  });
const { cellOptions: ageCellOptions, validation: ageValidation } =
  useTableCellInput(tableData, {
    label: 'Edad',
    placeholder: '18-120',
    emptyActionText: 'Agregar edad',
    type: 'number',
    rules: [
      {
        required: true,
        message: 'La edad es requerida',
        trigger: 'blur',
      },
      {
        type: 'number',
        transform: (value: string) => Number(value),
        message: 'Debe ser un número',
        trigger: 'blur',
      },
      {
        type: 'number',
        transform: (value: string) => Number(value),
        min: 18,
        max: 120,
        message: 'La edad debe estar entre 18 y 120',
        trigger: 'blur',
      },
    ],
    useRowIndex: true,
  });
const { cellOptions: websiteCellOptions, validation: websiteValidation } =
  useTableCellInput(tableData, {
    label: 'Sitio web',
    placeholder: 'https://ejemplo.com',
    emptyActionText: 'Agregar URL',
    rules: [
      {
        type: 'url',
        message: 'Ingresa una URL válida (ej: https://...)',
        trigger: 'blur',
      },
    ],
    useRowIndex: true,
  });
const clearAllValidations = () => {
  emailValidation?.clearAllValidation();
  ageValidation?.clearAllValidation();
  websiteValidation?.clearAllValidation();
};
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="mb-md">
      <g-button @click="clearAllValidations">Limpiar validaciones</g-button>
    </div>
    <g-table :data="tableData" border style="width: 100%" :cell-style="{ zIndex: 'auto' }">
      <g-table-column prop="name" label="Nombre" width="160" />
      <g-table-column
        prop="email"
        label="Email (required + email)"
        width="220"
        cell-type="input"
        :cell-options="emailCellOptions"
      />
      <g-table-column
        prop="age"
        label="Edad (18-120)"
        width="150"
        cell-type="input"
        :cell-options="ageCellOptions"
      />
      <g-table-column
        prop="website"
        label="Website (URL)"
        width="220"
        cell-type="input"
        :cell-options="websiteCellOptions"
      />
    </g-table>
    <div class="mt-md text-gray-500 text-3">
      <p><strong>Instrucciones:</strong> Haz click en una celda para editarla. Al salir (blur), se validará el contenido.</p>
      <p>Prueba dejando campos vacíos, ingresando emails inválidos, edades fuera de rango, o URLs mal formadas.</p>
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>

Eventos de celdas editables story ok 
<script lang="ts" setup>
import { useTableCellInput, useTableCellSelect } from "../components/table/index.ts";
import { ref } from "vue";
import { GConfigProvider } from '../components/config-provider';
import { GTable } from '../components/table/index.ts';
import { GTableColumn } from '../components/table/index.ts';

const tableData = ref([
  { id: 1, name: 'Tom', status: 'active', note: '' },
  { id: 2, name: 'Jane', status: 'inactive', note: '' },
]);
const eventsLog = ref<Array<{ event: string; payload: string }>>([]);
const maxLog = 12;
const pushLog = (event: string, ...args: unknown[]) => {
  const payload = args
    .map(a =>
      typeof a === 'object' && a !== null && 'property' in a
        ? `column.${(a as { property?: string }).property}`
        : String(a),
    )
    .join(', ');
  eventsLog.value = [{ event, payload }, ...eventsLog.value].slice(
    0,
    maxLog,
  );
};
const onCellEditOpen = (row: unknown, column: unknown) => {
  pushLog('cell-edit-open', row, column);
};
const onCellEditClose = (row: unknown, column: unknown) => {
  pushLog('cell-edit-close', row, column);
};
const onCellEditChange = (
  row: unknown,
  column: unknown,
  newVal: unknown,
  oldVal: unknown,
) => {
  pushLog(
    'cell-edit-change',
    row,
    column,
    String(oldVal) + ' -> ' + String(newVal),
  );
};
const onCellEditValidate = (
  row: unknown,
  column: unknown,
  result: { valid: boolean; message: string },
) => {
  pushLog(
    'cell-edit-validate',
    row,
    column,
    result.valid ? 'valid' : result.message,
  );
};
const statusOptions = [
  { value: 'active', title: 'Activo' },
  { value: 'inactive', title: 'Inactivo' },
];
const { cellOptions: statusCellOptions } = useTableCellSelect(tableData, {
  options: statusOptions,
  useRowIndex: true,
});
const { cellOptions: noteCellOptions } = useTableCellInput(tableData, {
  placeholder: 'Nota',
  useRowIndex: true,
});
</script>

<template>
  <g-config-provider>
    <div class="flex gap-md">
      <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <g-table
          :data="tableData"
          border
          style="width: 100%"
          :cell-style="{ zIndex: 'auto' }"
          @cell-edit-open="onCellEditOpen"
          @cell-edit-close="onCellEditClose"
          @cell-edit-change="onCellEditChange"
          @cell-edit-validate="onCellEditValidate"
        >
          <g-table-column prop="name" label="Nombre" width="120" />
          <g-table-column
            prop="status"
            label="Estado"
            width="140"
            cell-type="select"
            :cell-options="statusCellOptions"
          />
          <g-table-column
            prop="note"
            label="Nota"
            cell-type="input"
            :cell-options="noteCellOptions"
          />
        </g-table>
      </div>
      <div class="w-72 flex-shrink-0">
        <p class="font-medium text-3 mb-xs">Últimos eventos</p>
        <ul class="text-2 space-y-xxs text-gray-600">
          <li v-for="(entry, i) in eventsLog" :key="i" class="font-mono truncate" :title="entry.payload">
            <span class="text-primary-txt font-medium">{{ entry.event }}</span>
          </li>
          <li v-if="eventsLog.length === 0" class="text-gray-400">Interactúa con las celdas editables</li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  </g-config-provider>
</template>
